When using SSH to connect and authenticate to GitHub, you may need to troubleshoot unexpected issues that may arise.

- [Using SSH over the HTTPS port, 1 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/using-ssh-over-the-https-port)
- [Recovering your SSH key passphrase, 2 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/recovering-your-ssh-key-passphrase)
- [Deleted or missing SSH keys, 3 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/deleted-or-missing-ssh-keys)
- [Error: Host key verification failed, 4 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-host-key-verification-failed)
- [Error: Permission denied (publickey), 5 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-permission-denied-publickey)
- [Error: Bad file number, 6 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-bad-file-number)
- [Error: Key already in use, 7 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-key-already-in-use)
- [Error: Permission to user/repo denied to other-user, 8 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-permission-to-userrepo-denied-to-other-user)
- [Error: Permission to user/repo denied to user/other-repo, 9 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-permission-to-userrepo-denied-to-userother-repo)
- [Error: Agent admitted failure to sign, 10 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-agent-admitted-failure-to-sign)
- [Error: ssh-add: illegal option -- apple-use-keychain, 11 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-ssh-add-illegal-option----apple-use-keychain)
- [Error: SSL certificate problem, verify that the CA cert is OK, 12 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-ssl-certificate-problem-verify-that-the-ca-cert-is-ok)
- [Error: Unknown key type, 13 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-unknown-key-type)
- [Error: We're doing an SSH key audit, 14 of 14](https://docs.github.com/en/authentication/troubleshooting-ssh/error-were-doing-an-ssh-key-audit)
